Implementation details for full LSE gradient via FFT:
```
def lse_gradient_full(a, c, T, fft_len, n):
    z = c / T
    z -= z.max()
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = e.sum()
    if s <= 0: w = np.ones(len(c))/len(c)
    else: w = e/s
    # FFT correlation r_j = sum_u a_u w_{j+u}
    A = rfft(a, fft_len)
    W = rfft(w, fft_len)
    r = irfft(W * np.conj(A), fft_len)[:n]
    return T? value = T*(zmax+log(s)), grad = 2*r
```
We need fft_len >= L=2n-1 and maybe power of two. Since w length L, a padded to L. `rfft(a, fft_len)` with a length n zero-pads automatically? Numpy rfft(x, n) input length n? If x shorter, zero pads. Good. `w` length L; pass `w` and n=fft_len. Product length. Use `np.conj(A)`. Confirm with direct for random small maybe in code? Can't test here but can include optional debug? We'll trust or use direct topk to be safe? We can compute gradient direct for topk and full loop for n small? For n=1600, full direct O(nL) ~5M operations per iter, maybe okay but slower. We can verify formula in code at startup for random n=5: compare FFT corr to direct and if mismatch adjust. Since no external output? We can print. If mismatch, use direct method or choose correct. Let's include a small self-test function `test_corr_formula` maybe not too costly. But in final, if formula wrong, optimizer bad. Let's derive again with DFT to be certain.

We need r_j = ∑_{u} a_u w_{j+u mod L}, for j=0..n-1, with no wrap. DFT R_k=∑_j r_j e^{-2πikj/L}=∑_j∑_u a_u w_{j+u} e^{-2πikj/L}. Let l=j+u mod L, j=l-u mod L. e^{-ikj}=e^{-ik(l-u)}=e^{-ikl} e^{iku}. Sum_l w_l e^{-ikl}=W_k. Sum_u a_u e^{iku}=A_{-k}. For real a, A_{-k}=conj(A_k). So R_k = W_k * conj(A_k). Yes. Numpy's `rfft` returns X[0..N/2] for DFT with e^{-...}. `irfft(product, N)` computes inverse of conjugate-symmetric series. Need product for k=0..N/2 equal W_k conj(A_k). For real a,w, product's negative freqs conjugate: R_{N-k}=W_{N-k} conj(A_{N-k}) = conj(W_k) A_k = conj(W_k conj(A_k))? conj(W_k conj(A_k)) = conj(W_k) A_k. Yes. So `irfft(W * np.conj(A))` yields r. Good.

But wait if using `rfft(a, fft_len)` where a padded length L<fft_len, zeros, fine. `rfft(w, fft_len)` w length L. Good.

Optimizer z-Adam:
```
def optimize_z(a0, time_limit, T_start_factor, T_end_factor, lr_start, lr_end, n_stages, topk=None, seed=0, min_score_gap? no)
  n=len(a0); L=2n-1; fft=next_pow2(L)
  a=normalize(a0)
  z=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-30))
  best=a.copy(); best_score=score
  rng=...
  stages = n_stages
  for si in range(stages):
    frac=si/(stages-1); T=factor* cmax? Need cmax update.
    lr = interpolate log
    m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); t=0
    no_improve=0
    while time...:
      a=softmax(z)
      c, score, grad = conv_and_grad(a, fft_len, T, topk)
      if score < best_score: update.
      S = float(np.dot(a, grad))
      grad_z = a*(grad - S)
      # maybe scale grad_z by T? No grad wrt a of LSE already includes T.
      Adam update.
      z = z - lr * m_hat/(sqrt(v)+eps)
      z -= z.max()
      if no_improve > max_inters:
         # perturb/mix
```

Pseudo:
```
def lse_search(a0, time_limit, seed):
  best=a0; best_score=...
  t_end = t0+time_limit
  while time < t_end - 5:
     # choose init
     if random run: init = mix(best, noise)
     run_lse_for_time(init, slice_time, T_start, T_end, lr, topk)
     update best
  return best
```

Let's think about final peak shaving more. Once near optimum, the max coefficient is determined by a few (or many) active lags. We can do localized transfer:
- Compute c, active lags = indices where c > cmax*(1 - eps) or top K.
- For each active lag k and each contributing pair (i,j) with i+j=k, product a_i a_j. To reduce c_k, we can move mass from one of i/j to a destination that doesn't strongly increase other active lags. Previous did one source per lag. We can improve by:
   * For each pair, consider moving a fraction δ from i to i' or j to j'. Compute linear + quadratic change in active peaks. Choose move that maximally reduces max.
   * Use local coordinate pair optimization: For a move from src to dst, the new a differs in two coordinates. The change in c_l = 2 a_{dst-l} δ + 2 a_{src-l} δ - 2? Let's derive: a' = a - δ e_src + δ e_dst. c' = c + δ( (dst shift? ) ). Since c = a*a. Linear change: δ(conv with e_dst)*a + δ a*(conv with e_src) - 2δ^2 e_src e_dst? More precisely c'_l - c_l = δ(a_{l-dst}+a_{dst-l})? Wait self-conv with a' = a + δ(e_dst-e_src). c' = c + δ(u+u^T) + δ^2 d*d. For self-conv coefficient l: (c + 2δ( a_{l-dst} I_dstvalid + a_{l-src} I_srcvalid?)? Need use gradient: directional derivative = 2δ( (a shifted by dst) +? Since gradient of c wrt a_j = 2 a_{l-j} (for self-conv). So linear change = δ [2 a_{l-dst} - 2 a_{l-src}] (with valid). Quadratic = δ^2 if l = src+src? Actually d=e_dst-e_src, d*d coefficient at l: 1 if l=dst+dst, -2 if l=src+dst, +1 if l=src+src. So can compute exact for top lags. Use line search for δ. We can sort candidate moves by reduction in current max. This can polish better.

Implementing exact move evaluation for many candidates may be expensive but n=1600, top lags ~100. Candidate sources/destinations limited.

Simpler improved peak shaving:
- Maintain a sorted list of active lags `active` (top ~100 or those >0.9 cmax).
- For each active lag k, find contributing pairs. For each source i (and maybe j) with high product, choose destination dst among low-contribution positions (not in danger) and fraction. But use exact linear approximate: compute delta_max for a small move; choose best. Then apply full line search over fractions `[0.001,0.005,0.01,...]` computing c via FFT (fast). This is similar previous but can consider both i and j as sources simultaneously and target destinations based on "danger scores".
- Danger score for destination d: how much increasing a_d increases active peaks: D_d = ∑_{k active} w_k * 2 a_{k-d}. We want low D_d. Source score: reducing a_s decreases active peaks: S_s = ∑ w_k*2 a_{k-s}. Candidate move value ≈ D_d - S_s (increase). We want negative (decrease). Also consider moving from src to dst changes sum? normalize maybe negligible. Use ratio. This is a linearized subgradient step but coordinate-wise; more efficient than trying many fracs.
- Then perform move with line search.

This may be a strong final polish.

Another final method: "Differential evolution of moves": use gradient z and do random coordinate pairs.

Let's consider using the exact KKT active set with linear programming over moves: At optimum, for each i with a_i>0, the directional derivative for moving from i to j should be >=0 for any j (otherwise improve). Our peak shaving can enforce.

Could also use projected stochastic gradient to converge further.

Now, let's detail LSE optimizer.

`softmax_fn(z)`: stable.
```
def softmax_fn(z):
    z = z - np.max(z)
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = e.sum()
    if s < 1e-300: return np.ones(len(z))/len(z)
    return e/s
```
`normalize_positive(a, floor=0.0)`:
```
arr=np.maximum(np.asarray(a,float),0); s=arr.sum(); if s<1e-15: return ones/n; return arr/s
```

`next_pow2(m)`:
```
def next_pow2(m):
    if m <=1: return 1
    return 1 << (m-1).bit_length()
```

`convolve_and_gradient(a, fft_len, T, topk)`:
Need handle topk with k maybe None or int. If topk <=0? None.
```
def convolve_and_gradient(a, fft_len, T, topk=None):
    n=len(a); L=2*n-1
    A=rfft(a, fft_len)
    c=irfft(A*A, fft_len)[:L]
    c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
    cmax=float(c.max())
    score=2.0*n*cmax
    if topk is None:
        k=L
    else:
        k=min(max(1,int(topk)), L)
    if k >= L:
       z=c/T; zmax=z.max(); expz=np.exp(z-zmax); s=expz.sum(); w=expz/s
    else:
       top_idx=np.argpartition(c, -k)[-k:] # if k==L? handle
       vals=c[top_idx]
       z=vals/T; zmax=z.max(); expz=np.exp(z-zmax); s=expz.sum(); wtop=expz/s
       w=np.zeros(L); w[top_idx]=wtop
    W=rfft(w, fft_len)
    r=irfft(W * np.conj(A), fft_len)[:n]
    grad=2.0*r
    val=T*(zmax+np.log(s))
    return c, score, grad, val, cmax
```
Potential issue `np.argpartition(c, -k)` with k=L may error? handle. For k=L, no need.

If T very small and topk values have max diff, okay. If c all zero (a zero) score 0? Not possible sum=1.

Let's design a `pair_move_polish` more targeted:
For current a,c:
- active_idx = top 20 peaks or threshold.
- For each active lag k, compute pair products for j range. The top product index `j*` (source) and partner `k-j*`. To reduce this peak, we can move a small amount from one of the two to a destination `d` with low "danger" for active peaks.
- Danger for destination d `danger[d] = ∑_{k active} w_k * (a_{k-d})` (increase if add). Source savings `save[src] = ∑ w_k * (a_{k-src})` (decrease if remove). Move value = danger[d] - save[src]. Choose min.
- Since moving from src to dst also changes pair product at lag src+dst and src+src/dst+dst; exact evaluation handles.

```
def basis_score(coeffs, M):
    t = (np.arange(M)+0.5)/M
    p = np.ones(M)
    for m in range(1, len(coeffs)+1):
        p += coeffs[m-1]*np.cos(np.pi*m*t)
    p = np.maximum(p,0)
    s=p.sum()
    if s<1e-12: return 1e6
    a=p/s
    ff=next_pow2(2*M-1)
    c=selfconv_with_fft(a, ff)
    return 2.0*M*c.max()
```
Use optimizer with bounds e.g. [-1.5,1.5] to keep p positive maybe. If p has zeros okay. The score can be >2 for bad.

Potential issue: Full LSE with T too small and Adam in z-space may converge to a local minimum with some zeros? Since z can go to -inf, a entries zero. That's allowed. We can add optional L2 entropy penalty: minimize LSE(c/T) + λ * ∑ a_i log a_i? Gradient includes λ. This encourages positive support and may help escape. But objective score primary. Could include small `ent_lambda` in gradient: `grad_a += λ * (log a + 1)`? But derivative of ∑ a log a = log a +1. On simplex, entropy term negative encourages uniform. We can add tiny, e.g. λ=1e-4 cmax? Scaling tricky. Maybe not.

Instead, in z-space, add small bias to grad_z that pushes toward log uniform: `grad_z += ent_coeff * (a - 1/n)`. This is gradient of entropy penalization `(ent_coeff/2)∑(a-1/n)^2`? Could stabilize. Use very small (1e-6). Or note full LSE with high T smooths.

Need tune lr. We can implement automatic lr control:
- Use baseline lr schedule. Track `score` and `lse_val` maybe. If no improvement for 500 iters, reduce lr by 0.5 (down to min). If improvement recent, maybe increase slightly. Simple.
- Adam with lr in z units. Start 0.05, end 0.005.

Let's write `run_z_lse` with parameters and return best. Pseudocode:
```
def run_z_lse(a0, time_limit, T_start_rel, T_end_rel, lr_start, lr_end, topk, seed, init_mix=0.0, restart_interval=...):
    n=len(a0); a=normalize(a0); L=2*n-1; ff=next_pow2(L)
    best=a.copy(); c0=fast_selfconv(a,ff); best_score=score...
    z=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-20)) - np.log(n)
    rng=...
    t0=time.monotonic(); t_end=t0+time_limit
    m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); adam_t=0
    it=0
    no_improve=0
    lr_scale=1.0
    # perhaps stages by time fraction
    while time < t_end-1:
        a=softmax(z)
        c,max,grad = conv_grad(a,ff,T_rel*(cmax_ref), topk)
        if score < best_score - 1e-12: best=... no_improve=0; cmax_ref=max
        else: no_improve+=1
        if no_improve == 500: lr_scale = max(0.25, lr_scale*0.7); no_improve=0
        if no_improve > 5000: # restart from best with noise
            scale=0.05+0.2*min(1, it/10000)
            a_p=perturb(best, rng, scale)
            z=np.log(np.maximum(a_p,1e-20))-np.log(n)
            m=v=0; adam_t=0; lr_scale=1
            no_improve=0
            continue
        # Adam
        S=np.dot(a,grad); grad_z=a*(grad-S)
        # optional small uniform regularization
        # grad_z += 1e-8 * (a - 1/n)?
        lr = (lr_start+(lr_end-lr_start)*frac) * lr_scale
        update
        z -= lr * ...
        z -= z.max()
        it+=1
    # end: also evaluate softmax(z)
    return best, best_score
```
Need `T_rel` dynamic. We can compute `frac = (time.monotonic()-t0)/time_limit`. But if we use cmax_ref dynamic, T = T_rel(t)*cmax_ref. Use `cmax_ref = max(cmax, 1e-12)`.
For topk, if `topk` is None, topk=None. If int, maybe decay to smaller? Parameter `topk` can be int or None. For multi run, can use `topk_start` and `topk_end`; simplify pass fixed.

Maybe T_rel_start=1.0 means T=cmax. Full softmax nearly uniform over all coefficients? Since c range [~0.5/n,0.75/n], cmax=0.75/n, T=0.75/n, weights not too concentrated. final 0.02*cmax.

Topk fixed e.g. 50. With topk, softmax over top 50 with T_rel*cmax. Use topk=100 for full-ish.

We can function `run_lse_schedule(a0, time_limit, name, T_start_rel, T_end_rel, lr_start, lr_end, topk, seed, max_restarts=4)` that performs multiple internal restarts within its time limit, using best as seed. Instead of one continuous run. For example: slices of 30s with increasing aggressiveness.

But calling function many times with small time may overhead. Let's implement internal restarts as above.

Now, coordinate polish details.

`coordinate_polish(a0, time_limit, seed)`:
```
n; a=normalize; best; c=fast; score
t0; stall=0
while time<limit-1:
   c, cmax, score = ...
   if score<best: update; stall=0
   else stall++
   if stall>200: random perturb or mix best; if no improve 1000 break
   # weights
   K=max(10, min(100, n//16))
   top_idx=argpartition(c,-K)[-K:]
   vals=c[top_idx]
   T_w=max(1e-12, 0.05*cmax)
   wtop=softmax((vals-cmax)/T_w) # negative values -> weights near max? Actually vals-cmax <=0; if T small, top weights large.
   w=zeros(L); w[top_idx]=wtop; w/=w.sum()
   # danger S = corr(w,a)
   A=rfft(a,ff); W=rfft(w,ff); S=irfft(W*conj(A),ff)[:n]
   # source candidates: high S and a>min
   min_a = 1e-14
   src_candidates = indices where a>min_a; top 30 by S.
   # dest candidates: low S, not src, maybe top 50 bottom by S
   all_idx = np.argsort(S) # low first
   dest_candidates = first 80 excluding src and maybe a<...? Could include high a if low danger.
   # Create pairs
   best_linear=0; best_move=None
   for sidx in src_candidates[:20]:
      for didx in dest_candidates[:40]:
         if didx==sidx: continue
         val = S[didx] - S[sidx]
         if val < best_linear-1e-15:
             ...
   # Try top 20 pairs by val. We can collect and sort.
   for each pair:
      for frac in [0.002,0.005,0.01,0.02,0.05,0.1,0.25,0.5]:
         amt=a[src]*frac
         a2=a.copy(); a2[src]-=amt; a2[dst]+=amt; a2=np.maximum(a2,0); s2=a2.sum(); if s2<: continue; a2/=s2
         c2=fast; score2=...
         if score2 < score - 1e-12: accept; break
   if no accept: 
      if stall>100: random small move or perturb
      if stall>800: break
```
This may be slow if 20 pairs*8 fracs*FFT =160 per loop; each FFT 1ms =>0.16s, maybe 20 loops/s, 300s many. Could be okay but we can reduce fracs after first improvement. Use fractions [0.01,0.05,0.2,0.5]. 80 FFT. Good.

Could use `scipy.optimize.linprog` to solve LP best response for Nesterov a few times. This exact operator may find different. Let's consider implementing a simple Nesterov with approximate best response via projected gradient; no cvxpy. We can run it as a heuristic.

Game idea from previous: For fixed y, solve b* = argmin_{b∈Δ} max_l (y*b)_l (convex). Then Nesterov updates:
```
y_{t+1}= (1-α)y_t + α b_t
a_{t+1}= y_{t+1} + (t/(t+2))*(y_{t+1}-y_t) clipped normalized
```
At end, a may be good. We can approximate b* with `run_convex_max_response(y, inner_time=0.05?)` using LSE gradient for fixed y (linear objective). Since y fixed, c(y,b)=conv(y,b). Gradient wrt b for LSE with weights w: `grad_b_j = ∑_k w_k y_{k-j}` (no factor 2? For linear y*b, derivative wrt b_j = y_{k-j}; if using LSE, gradient). This is correlation of w and y. Use mirror descent in z-space for b until time slice.

Algorithm `best_response_convex(y, time_limit, T_rel, lr)`: minimize logsumexp(conv(y,b)/T). Since convex, can use smaller time and many iters. Return b.
Then `nesterov_game(a0, time_limit, inner_iters?)`:
- y=a0, b uniform or y.
- t=1
- while time:
   b=best_response_convex(y, inner_time=0.02? maybe fixed 20 iterations)
   α=2/(t+2)

Nesterov game using LP:
```
def nesterov_lp_game(a0, time_limit, solver_iters=None):
    n; y=a; a_nest=y; warm? no
    t=1; best...
    last_restarts=...
    while time < limit-5:
       T=build_toeplitz_dense(y)
       A_ub = np.concatenate([T, -np.ones((L,1))], axis=1)
       # maybe reduce constraints: only lags with? all.
       res=linprog(c_obj, A_ub=A_ub, b_ub=zeros(L), A_eq=A_eq, b_eq=[1], bounds=bounds)
       if res.success: b=res.x[:n]; clip normalize; M=res.x[-1]
       else break/restart
       alpha=2/(t+2)
       y_new=(1-alpha)y+alpha b; y_new=normalize
       if t==1: a_new=b
       else:
          momentum=(t-1)/(t+2)
          a_new=y_new + momentum*(y_new-y)
          a_new=clip normalize
       y=y_new; a=a_new
       update best for a,y, maybe averages
       t+=1
       if t%100==0 and time stall restart
    return best
```

```
def basis_score(coeffs, M):
    t = (np.arange(M, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(M)
    p = np.ones(M, dtype=np.float64)
    for m in range(1, len(coeffs)+1):
        p += coeffs[m-1] * np.cos(np.pi * m * t)
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    s = p.sum()
    if s < 1e-12:
        return 1e6
    a = p / s
    ff = next_pow2(2*M-1)
    c = fast_selfconv(a, ff)
    sc = 2.0 * M * c.max()
    return float(sc)
```
This ignores if p has large coefficient causing score >? okay.

Could use `cos_terms = [np.cos(pi*m*t) for m]` within loop; O(dim*M).

Maybe include a penalty for negative before clipping? Clipping changes normalization. But okay.

Pseudo `run_z_lse` in code:
```
def run_z_lse(a0, time_limit, T_start_rel, T_end_rel, lr_start, lr_end, topk, seed=0, use_best_restart=True):
    if time_limit <= 1: return normalize(a0), inf? 
    n=len(a0); L=2*n-1; ff=next_pow2(L)
    a=normalize_positive(a0)
    best=a.copy()
    c=fast_selfconv(a,ff); best_score=2*n*c.max()
    cmax_ref=float(c.max())
    z=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300))
    z -= np.mean? Use shift: z = z - z.max()
    m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); t_adam=0
    t0=time.monotonic(); t_end=t0+time_limit
    it=0; no_improve=0; lr_scale=1.0
    # maybe use local n_float
    for it in range(10000000):
        elapsed=time.monotonic()-t0
        if elapsed > time_limit - 1: break
        a=softmax_fn(z)
        # if a sum not 1? softmax does
        c, score, grad, _, cmax = conv_grad(a, ff, T_rel*cmax_ref, topk)
        if score < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score=score; best=a.copy(); cmax_ref=max(score/(2*n),1e-12); no_improve=0
        else:
            no_improve += 1
        if no_improve == 300:
            lr_scale = max(0.2, lr_scale*0.75)
            no_improve = 0
        if no_improve > 4000 and use_best_restart:
            # restart
            scale = 0.05 + 0.15*min(1.0, float(it)/20000)
            a_p = perturb_sequence(best, rng, scale)
            z = np.log(np.maximum(a_p,1e-300)); z -= z.max()
            m.fill(0); v.fill(0); t_adam=0; lr_scale=1.0; no_improve=0
            continue
        S = float(np.dot(a, grad))
        grad_z = a * (grad - S)
        # optional small regularization to uniform
        # grad_z += 1e-9 * (a - 1.0/n)
        frac = elapsed / max(time_limit,1e-6)
        lr = (lr_start + (lr_end - lr_start)*frac) * lr_scale
        t_adam += 1
        m = 0.9*m + 0.1*grad_z
        v = 0.999*v + 0.001*grad_z*grad_z
        m_hat = m / (1 - 0.9**t_adam)
        v_hat = v / (1 - 0.999**t_adam)
        z = z - lr * m_hat / (np.sqrt(v_hat) + 1e-8)
        z -= z.max()
    a_final=softmax_fn(z); c=fast...; score...
    if score<best: update
    return best, best_score
```
Need `rng` created inside. `topk` can be None or int. If topk is None and full LSE, `conv_grad` uses full. If topk int, use topk. T_rel computed with `cmax_ref`. Simpler: inside after c computed, `T = max(1e-15, T_rel * cmax)`. But T_rel changes with time. We need cmax before computing grad; we can compute c first then W. That requires two FFT? We can compute A, c, cmax, then W. In `conv_grad`, T can be computed inside from cmax. Better function `conv_grad_dynamic(a, ff, T_rel, topk)`:
```
A=rfft(a,ff); c=...; cmax=c.max(); T=max(1e-15, T_rel*cmax); ...
```
Then no need cmax_ref. T adapts to current cmax. Good. But if cmax changes abruptly, T adapts. Use T_rel based on time.

Need `correlation_weights(weights, a, ff)` helper:
```
def correlation_weights(w, a, ff):
    W=rfft(w,ff); A=rfft(a,ff); return irfft(W*np.conj(A),ff)[:len(a)]
```
Could use in LSE gradient too. But LSE uses A already.

`fast_selfconv` with ff:
```
def fast_selfconv(a, ff=None):
    n=len(a); L=2*n-1
    if ff is None: ff=next_pow2(L)
    A=rfft(a,ff)
    c=irfft(A*A,ff)[:L]
    return np.maximum(c,0)
```

Now, `nesterov_lp_game` implementation details.

Potential large memory, use try/except MemoryError. Use `linprog` if available:
```

   # Anneal random restarts: if no improve many iters, add phase noise or reset target ratio.
```

`update_phase_half(C_half, A_half)`:
```
def adjust_phase_half(C_half, A_half):
    magC = np.abs(C_half)
    magA = np.abs(A_half)
    # where magA small, phase 0
    phiA = np.angle(A_half)
    argC = np.angle(C_half)
    ang1 = 0.5 * argC
    ang2 = ang1 + np.pi
    two_pi = 2*np.pi
    diff1 = np.abs(((phiA - ang1 + np.pi) % two_pi) - np.pi)
    diff2 = np.abs(((phiA - ang2 + np.pi) % two_pi) - np.pi)
    phi = np.where(diff1 <= diff2, ang1, ang2)
    # k=0: C0 positive real, choose positive if A0 positive else maybe sign? A0 = sum a positive, so positive. Set phi[0]=0.
    phi[0]=0.0
    if len(C_half) == ff//2+1 and ff % 2 ==0:
       # Nyquist real; choose sign matching A_half[-1].real
       if A_half[-1].real < 0: phi[-1]=np.pi
       else: phi[-1]=0.0
    out = np.sqrt(magC) * np.exp(1j*phi)
    out[0] = np.sqrt(max(float(C_half[0].real),0.0))
    if ff % 2 == 0:
       # Nyquist should be real; set sign to match current A magnitude
       sign = -1.0 if A_half[-1].real < 0 else 1.0
       out[-1] = sign * np.sqrt(max(abs(C_half[-1]),0.0))
    return out
```
Need know ff to handle Nyquist. Pass `ff`.

`half_to_full(A_half, ff)`:
```
def half_to_full(A_half, ff):
    A_full = np.empty(ff, dtype=np.complex128)
    A_full[:ff//2+1] = A_half
    if ff % 2 == 0:
        # for k=1..ff/2-1
        A_full[ff//2+1:] = np.conj(A_half[1:ff//2][::-1])
    else:
        A_full[ff//2+1:] = np.conj(A_half[1:][::-1])
    return A_full
```

For even, last half positions from ff//2+1 to ff-1 correspond k=ff//2-1 down to1. Number = ff//2-1. `A_half[1:ff//2]` length ff//2-1 (1..ff//2-1). Reverse gives ff//2-1,...,1. Good. For ff=8, A_half[1:4]=1,2,3? Wait ff//2=4, slice 1:4 =>1,2,3 length3; but Nyquist index 4? Actually ff//2+1=5 half includes 0,1,2,3,4. Nyquist is 4. For even, non-Nyquist positive are 1..3 (ff//2-1=3). `A_half[1:ff//2]` = 1:4 =>1,2,3. Good. Reverse 3,2,1 assign to indices5,6,7 -> k=3,2,1. correct.
For odd ff=7, half length4 indices0,1,2,3. Negative indices6=conj1,5=conj2,4=conj3. `A_half[1:]`=1,2,3 reverse. Good.

Function top-level:
```
def clip_to_box_sum(c, M):
    T = c.copy()
    for _ in range(20):
       excess = T - M; pos = excess>0
       if not pos.any(): break
       ex = excess[pos].sum()
       if ex < 1e-14: break
       T[pos] -= excess[pos]
       deficit = 1.0 - T.sum()
       if abs(deficit) < 1e-14: break
       room = M - T
       room = np.maximum(room,0)
       rs = room.sum()
       if rs < 1e-14:
           s=T.sum(); T/=s if s>0 else uniform; break
       add = deficit * room / rs
       T += add
    T = np.maximum(T,0)
    s=T.sum(); if s>1e-14: T/=s else ...
    return T
```
This is O(L*20) per iter, fine.

Implement `fourier_flat_search` with internal iterations and time. It will compute score each iter. It may output progress.

Pseudo code with details:
```
def fourier_flat_search(a0, time_limit, ratio_end=1.25, seed=0):
    if time_limit < 5: ...
    n=len(a0); L=2*n-1; ff=next_pow2(L)
    a=normalize(a0)
    best=a.copy()
    # compute best
    best_score=score...
    cmean=1.0/(2*n-1)
    A_half=rfft(a,ff) # method pads? rfft(a,ff) with a length n zero pads.
    c=irfft(A_half*A_half,ff)[:L]; cmax=...
    ratio_init=max(1.0, cmax/cmean)
    rng=...
    t0=time.monotonic(); t_end=t0+time_limit; it=0; no_improve=0; noise_level=0.05
    while time < t_end -1:
       it +=1
       elapsed=...
       frac=elapsed/time_limit
       target_ratio = ratio_init + (ratio_end - ratio_init)*min(1,frac*1.2 maybe)
       if target_ratio < 1.05: target_ratio=1.05
       M = cmean*target_ratio
       # current c from A_half (already)
       c = np.maximum(irfft(A_half*A_half,ff)[:L],0)
       cmax=c.max(); score=2*n*cmax
       if score < best_score: best=a; ...
       else no_improve++
       if no_improve > 1000:
          # add random phase noise to A_half or restart
          if it % 2 ==0:
             a = best copy? or perturb
             A_half = rfft(a,ff)
             c...
          no_improve=0
       T = clip_to_box_sum(c, M)
       C = rfft(np.pad? T) # T length L, need pad to ff: Tp=T; Tp=np.zeros(ff); Tp[:L]=T
       Anew = adjust_phase_half(C, A_half, ff)
       # occasional phase noise
       if (it % 100 == 0):
          # multiply random phases small
          if ff > 4:
             noise = rng.normal(0, noise_level, size=ff//2+1)
             noise[0]=0.0
             if ff%2==0: noise[-1]=rng.choice([-1,1])? For Nyq sign maybe random flip
             Anew = Anew * np.exp(1j*noise)
             Anew[0]=abs(Anew[0])
             if ff%2==0: Anew[-1]=abs(Anew[-1]) * (1 if rng.random()>0.5 else -1)
       a_full = irfft(Anew, ff)
       pos = np.maximum(a_full, 0)
       # window sums over ff
       pos2 = np.concatenate((pos,pos))
       cs = np.concatenate(([0.0], np.cumsum(pos2)))
       sums = cs[n:n+ff] - cs[:ff]
       s_idx = int(np.argmax(sums))
       a_new = np.roll(pos, -s_idx)[:n]
       sn = a_new.sum()
       if sn < 1e-10:
          a_new = a
       else:
          a_new /= sn
       # evaluate
       A_half = rfft(a_new, ff)
       c_new = np.maximum(irfft(A_half*A_half,ff)[:L],0)
       score_new=2*n*c_new.max()
       a = a_new  # set current to new even if score worse? Maybe if score_new < score*1.5 or finite
       if score_new < best_score:
          best=a_new.copy(); best_score=score_new
          print
       # optional if worst: a=best
       if not np.isfinite(score_new) or score_new > best_score*10:
          a=best.copy(); A_half=rfft(a,ff)
    return best, best_score
```

Let's implement `adjust_phase_half` carefully:
```
def adjust_phase_half(C_half, A_half, ff):
    magC = np.abs(C_half)
    # protect negative zero
    sqrtC = np.sqrt(magC)
    argC = np.angle(C_half)
    argA = np.angle(A_half)
    ang1 = 0.5 * argC
    ang2 = ang1 + np.pi
    two_pi = 2.0 * np.pi
    diff1 = np.abs(((argA - ang1 + np.pi) % two_pi) - np.pi)
    diff2 = np.abs(((argA - ang2 + np.pi) % two_pi) - np.pi)
    phi = np.where(diff1 <= diff2, ang1, ang2)
    out = sqrtC * np.exp(1j * phi)
    # force k=0 real nonnegative
    out[0] = float(np.sqrt(max(float(np.real(C_half[0])), 0.0)))
    if ff % 2 == 0:
        # Nyquist real; choose sign that best matches A_half[-1]
        sign = -1.0 if float(np.real(A_half[-1])) < 0.0 else 1.0
        out[-1] = sign * float(np.sqrt(max(float(np.abs(C_half[-1])), 0.0)))
    # For any frequencies with magC==0, set out=0 (already)
    return out
```
But if C_half[0] can be tiny negative due round? use real. Good.

Now, verify LSE gradient with full weights for self-conv. For full LSE in z-space, gradient wrt a of LSE(c/T) is `T * ∑_k (e_k/T) * ∂c_k/∂a = ∑ e_k ∂c_k`. ∂c_k/∂a_j = 2 a_{k-j}. Our `conv_grad` returns `2*r`, where r_j=∑_k w_k a_{k-j}, w=e/sum; but LSE gradient should be `∑ exp(c_k/T) * 2 a_{k-j}`, not weighted by e/sum. Wait! In `conv_grad`, we set w=exp/sum. But LSE derivative: d/d a [T log ∑ exp(c/T)] = T * (1/S)∑ exp(c/T) * (1/T) ∂c_k = (1/S)∑ exp(c_k/T) ∂c_k. Ah yes the 1/S! So derivative is weighted by softmax w, not raw exp. Correct: T cancels 1/T and leaves 1/S. So `grad = ∑ w_k 2 a_{k-j}`. Good. If using topk, w over topk only? For top-k log-sum-exp over selected k, same. Good.

For LSE, gradient sum scale ~? w sum1. Good.

`next_pow2`:
```
def next_pow2(x):
    if x <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (int(x) - 1).bit_length()
```
For x=2, (1).bit_length=1 =>2. x=1=>1. x=3199: (3198).bit_length=12? 2^11=2048,2^12=4096, (3198).bit_length=12 =>4096. Good.

`normalize_positive`:
```
def normalize_positive(a):
    arr=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
    arr=np.maximum(arr,0.0)
    s=arr.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-15:
        return np.ones(len(arr), dtype=np.float64)/max(1, len(arr))
    return arr/s
```
If arr empty? avoid.

`fast_selfconv`:
```
def fast_selfconv(a, ff=None):
    n=len(a)
    L=2*n-1
    if ff is None:
       ff=next_pow2(L)
    A=rfft(a, ff)
    c=irfft(A*A, ff)[:L]
    c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
    return c
```
Numpy `rfft(a,ff)` if a length n zero pads to ff. Good.

```

`perturb_sequence`:
```
def perturb_sequence(a, rng, scale):
    a=normalize_positive(a)
    b = a * (1.0 + rng.normal(0.0, scale, len(a)))
    b = np.maximum(b,0.0)
    s=b.sum()
    if s<1e-15: return a.copy()
    return b/s
```

`interpolate_sequence`:
```
def interpolate_sequence(a, new_n):
    a=normalize_positive(a)
    old_n=len(a)
    if old_n == new_n: return a.copy()
    old_x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,old_n)
    new_x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,new_n)
    b=np.interp(new_x, old_x, a)
    b=np.maximum(b,0)
    s=b.sum(); if s<... return ones/new_n; return b/s
```

`convolve_and_gradient_rel`:
```
def convolve_and_gradient_rel(a, ff, T_rel, topk=None):
    n=len(a); L=2*n-1
    A=rfft(a, ff)
    c=irfft(A*A, ff)[:L]
    c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
    cmax=float(c.max())
    if cmax <=0: cmax = 1e-15
    T = max(1e-15, float(T_rel) * cmax)
    if topk is None:
       z = c / T
       zmax = float(z.max())
       ez = np.exp(z - zmax)
       se = float(ez.sum())
       if se <= 0: w = np.ones(L)/L
       else: w = ez / se
    else:
       k = int(topk)
       if k <= 0: k=1
       if k >= L:
          # full
       else:
          idx = np.argpartition(c, -k)[-k:] if k < L else np.arange(L)
          vals = c[idx]
          z=vals/T; zmax=...; ez; se; wtop=ez/se
          w=np.zeros(L); w[idx]=wtop
    W=rfft(w, ff)
    r=irfft(W * np.conj(A), ff)[:n]
    grad=2.0*r
    val = T*(zmax+np.log(se))
    return c, float(2.0*n*cmax), grad, float(val)
```
If topk path, `zmax` and `se` need defined. For `argpartition` with k maybe L? handle. Use `if k >= L: ...`.

`run_z_lse`: Use helper. Need print progress maybe every 5s.
```
def run_z_lse(a0, time_limit, T_start_rel, T_end_rel, lr_start, lr_end, topk=None, seed=0):
    if time_limit <= 1: return normalize_positive(a0), inf? return score
    n=len(a0); L=2*n-1; ff=next_pow2(L)
    a=normalize_positive(a0)
    c=fast_selfconv(a,ff); best_score=2*n*c.max(); best=a.copy()
    z=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300))
    z -= z.max()
    m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); t_adam=0
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    t0=time.monotonic(); t_end=t0+time_limit
    it=0; no_improve=0; lr_scale=1.0
    # maybe store last score
    while time.monotonic() < t_end - 0.5:
       a=softmax_fn(z)
       frac = max(0.0, min(1.0, (time.monotonic()-t0)/time_limit))
       T_rel = T_start_rel + (T_end_rel - T_start_rel)*frac
       c, score, grad, val = convolve_and_gradient_rel(a, ff, T_rel, topk)
       if score < best_score - 1e-12:
          best_score=score; best=a.copy(); no_improve=0
          if it % 2000 ==0: print...
       else:
          no_improve += 1
       if no_improve == 300:
          lr_scale = max(0.2, lr_scale*0.7)
          no_improve = 0
       if no_improve > 5000:
          # restart from best with perturbation
          scale = 0.05 + 0.25*min(1.0, frac)
          a_p = perturb_sequence(best, rng, scale)
          z = np.log(np.maximum(a_p,1e-300)); z -= z.max()
          m.fill(0); v.fill(0); t_adam=0; lr_scale=1.0; no_improve=0
          continue
       S = float(np.dot(a, grad))
       # optional uniform regularization tiny
       grad_z = a * (grad - S)
       lr = (lr_start + (lr_end - lr_start)*frac) * lr_scale
       t_adam += 1
       m = 0.9*m + 0.1*grad_z
       v = 0.999*v + 0.001*grad_z*grad_z
       m_hat = m / (1 - 0.9**t_adam)
       v_hat = v / (1 - 0.999**t_adam)
       z = z - lr * m_hat / (np.sqrt(v_hat) + 1e-8)
       z -= z.max()
       it += 1
    # final evaluate
    return best, best_score
```

`clip_to_box_sum`:
```
def clip_to_box_sum(c, M):
    T = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if M <= 0:
       return T/T.sum()
    for _ in range(30):
        excess = T - M
        pos = excess > 0
        if not pos.any():
            break
        ex = float(excess[pos].sum())
        if ex < 1e-14:
            break
        T[pos] -= excess[pos]
        deficit = 1.0 - float(T.sum())
        if abs(deficit) < 1e-14:
            break
        room = M - T
        room = np.maximum(room, 0.0)
        rs = float(room.sum())
        if rs < 1e-14:
            break
        T += deficit * room / rs
    T = np.maximum(T, 0.0)
    s = T.sum()
    if s < 1e-14:
        return np.ones_like(T) / len(T)
    return T / s
```
If total capacity enough, converges. After final normalize, max may slightly >M? Accept.

Phase noise function top-level? Could implement inline but no lambda. We can define `add_phase_noise(A_half, rng, level, ff)` top-level.
```
def add_phase_noise(A_half, rng, level, ff):
    if len(A_half) <= 1: return A_half
    noise = rng.normal(0.0, level, len(A_half))
    noise[0] = 0.0
    if ff % 2 == 0:
        # Nyquist random sign flip with small prob? Instead set noise[-1]=0 to keep real; level 0.
        noise[-1] = 0.0
    A_half = A_half.copy()
    A_half *= np.exp(1j*noise)
    A_half[0] = abs(A_half[0])
    if ff % 2 == 0:
        A_half[-1] = float(A_half[-1].real)  # keep real, maybe sign lost; okay
    return A_half
```

def correlation_of_weights(w, a, ff):

```
def coordinate_polish(a0, time_limit, seed=0):
    n=len(a0); L=2*n-1; ff=next_pow2(L)
    a=normalize_positive(a0)
    c=fast_selfconv(a,ff); current_score=2*n*c.max(); best=a.copy(); best_score=current_score
    rng=...
    t0=time.monotonic(); t_end=t0+time_limit; stall=0
    while time< t_end -0.5:
       # maybe use current c
       cmax=c.max()

       uniq.sort(key=lambda??? lambda not allowed. Use tuple sort by val? `uniq.sort()` sorts by val then s d since tuples. Good.
       uniq=uniq[:12]
       accepted=False
       for val,s,d in uniq:
           if a[s] <= 1e-15: continue
           for frac in (0.005,0.02,0.08,0.2,0.5):
               amt=a[s]*frac

def nesterov_lp_game(a0, time_limit, seed=0):
    if time_limit < 30 or linprog is None: return normalize_positive(a0), score
    n=len(a0); L=2*n-1
    if n > 2000: return ... # too slow
    y=normalize_positive(a0)
    a_nest=y.copy()
    best=a_nest.copy()
    c=fast_selfconv(best); best_score=...
    Tmat=np.zeros((L,n+1)); Tmat[:,n] = -1.0
    c_obj=np.zeros(n+1); c_obj[-1]=1.0
    b_ub=np.zeros(L)
    A_eq=np.ones((1,n+1)); A_eq[0,n]=0.0
    b_eq=np.array([1.0],dtype=np.float64)
    bounds=[(0.0,None)]*(n+1)
    t0=time.monotonic(); t_end=t0+time_limit
    t=1; no_improve=0; fail=0
    while time < t_end-5:
       now=time.monotonic()
       # update Tmat
       for j in range(n):
           Tmat[j:j+n, j] = y[j]
       per_time = max(2.0, min(15.0, (t_end-now)*0.1))
       try:
          res=linprog(c_obj, A_ub=Tmat, b_ub=b_ub, A_eq=A_eq, b_eq=b_eq, bounds=bounds, method='highs', options={'time_limit': per_time, 'dual_feasibility_tolerance':1e-8, 'primal_feasibility_tolerance':1e-8})
       except Exception:
          res=None
       if res is None or not getattr(res, 'success', False):
          fail +=1
          if fail > 5: break
          continue
       b=res.x[:n].astype(np.float64); b=np.maximum(b,0); sb=b.sum(); if sb<1e-12: b=y.copy(); else b/=sb
       alpha=2.0/(t+2.0)
       y_new=(1-alpha)*y + alpha*b
       y_new=np.maximum(y_new,0); sy=y_new.sum(); if sy<1e-12: y_new=b; else y_new/=sy
       if t == 1:
          a_new=b.copy()
       else:
          momentum=(t-1)/(t+2)
          a_new = y_new + momentum*(y_new - y)
          a_new=np.maximum(a_new,0); sa=a_new.sum(); if sa<1e-12: a_new=y_new; else a_new/=sa
       y=y_new; a_nest=a_new
       # evaluate a_nest and y
       for candidate in (a_nest, y):
           c=fast_selfconv(candidate)
           sc=2*n*c.max()
           if sc < best_score - 1e-12: best=candidate.copy(); best_score=sc; no_improve=0
           else: no_improve+=1? careful double.
       # simpler eval a_nest and y separately
       ...
       if no_improve > 100:
           y=best.copy(); a_nest=best.copy(); t=1; no_improve=0; fail=0
       t +=1
    return best,best_score

```
def get_parent_sequence():
    try:
        seq = height_sequence_1
    except Exception:
        return None
    if seq is None: return None
    try: arr=np.asarray(seq,dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception: return None
    if arr.ndim !=1 or arr.size==0: return None
    arr=np.maximum(np.minimum(arr,1000.0),0.0)
    s=arr.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-10: return None
    return arr/s
```

```
def adjust_phase_half(C_half, A_half, ff):
    magC=np.abs(C_half); sqrtC=np.sqrt(magC)
    # product phase
    A2 = A_half * A_half
    # where |A2| > tiny
    with np.errstate(divide='ignore', invalid='ignore'):
        ratio = C_half / A2
    delta = np.angle(ratio)
    argA = np.angle(A_half)
    phi = argA + 0.5*delta
    # where |A2| small, use principal:
    small = np.abs(A2) < 1e-30
    if small.any(): phi[small] = 0.5*np.angle(C_half[small])
    out = sqrtC * np.exp(1j*phi)
    out[0]=sqrt(max(C0.real,0))
    if even Nyq: set real sign matching A (or delta should do, but force real)
```
This is better. If C/A2 ratio negative due branch, delta ~π, phi rotates by π/2? Wait if A^2 phase differs by π from C, half rotate π/2, but the two roots differ by π. Half rotation from current A to one of roots? If A^2 phase = argA*2; desired root phases are (argC±2πk)/2. If argC differs by π from 2argA, desired roots differ from argA by π/2 modulo π? But are they roots? Suppose A=1 (arg0), C=-1 (argπ). Roots are i and -i (arg π/2 or -π/2). Yes rotate by π/2. Good. If C=1, delta0, root A. Good. If current A not a root, this continuously moves phase toward nearest root. It may take iterations; with blur? We set magnitude sqrtC exactly each iter, phase half error. This is like Newton? Good.

Potential issue: If A2 magnitude very small but C not, use principal. Good.

Choose sign by `np.real(A_half[-1] * np.conj(sign*absC))` max, i.e., if real part of A * sign positive. Since sign ±1. Use `sign = 1 if np.real(A_half[-1] * np.sqrtC[-1]) >=0 else -1`? If A[-1] may have small imaginary; use real part. Good.

For k=0, A0 positive, C0 positive, out0=sqrt(C0). Good.

`adjust_phase_half`:
```
def adjust_phase_half(C_half, A_half, ff):
    magC = np.abs(C_half)
    sqrtC = np.sqrt(magC)
    A2 = A_half * A_half
    with np.errstate(divide='ignore', invalid='ignore'):
        ratio = C_half / A2
    delta = np.angle(ratio)
    argA = np.angle(A_half)
    phi = argA + 0.5 * delta
    small = np.abs(A_half) < 1e-15
    if np.any(small):
        phi[small] = 0.5 * np.angle(C_half[small])
    bad = ~np.isfinite(phi)
    if np.any(bad):
        phi[bad] = 0.5 * np.angle(C_half[bad])
    out = sqrtC * np.exp(1j * phi)
    out[0] = float(np.sqrt(max(float(np.real(C_half[0])), 0.0)))
    if ff % 2 == 0:
        nyq_raw = float(np.real(A_half[-1]))
        sign = -1.0 if nyq_raw < 0.0 else 1.0
        out[-1] = sign * float(np.sqrt(max(float(np.abs(C_half[-1])), 0.0)))
    bad_out = ~np.isfinite(out)
    if np.any(bad_out):
        out[bad_out] = 0.0
    return out
```
`np.isfinite` on complex returns bool per component? It works. But `bad_out = ~np.isfinite(out)` for complex returns bool array where either real/imag finite? yes. Assign `0.0` ok.
For Nyquist, if C magnitude zero, out=0. Good.

`add_phase_noise`:
```
def add_phase_noise(A_half, rng, level, ff):
    if len(A_half) <= 1:
        return A_half
    out = A_half.copy()
    noise = rng.normal(0.0, level, len(A_half))
    noise[0] = 0.0
    if ff % 2 == 0:
        noise[-1] = 0.0
    out = out * np.exp(1j * noise)
    out[0] = abs(out[0])
    if ff % 2 == 0:
        out[-1] = float(out[-1].real)
    return out
```
If `A_half` has zeros, fine.

Now, one possible bug: The `nesterov_lp_game` Tmat dimension `L=2*n-1`. For j from 0 to n-1, `Tmat[j:j+n, j] = y[j]` length n, end j+n. For j=n-1, end=2n-1 = L, valid slice up to L exclusive. Good.

`Tmat` last column -1. The A_ub constraints: for row l, ∑_j T[l,j] b_j - M <=0. T[l,j]=y[j] if j<=l<=j+n-1. This equals (y*b)_l? np.convolve(y,b)_l = ∑_j y[j] b[l-j] with b index 0..n-1. For fixed j, contribution at l=j+ b_index? Wait convolution matrix T should map b to y*b: (y*b)_l = ∑_{j=0}^{n-1} y_j b_{l-j} where b index valid 0..n-1. For a column j of b_j, it contributes to output row l = j + b_index? If column index is i (b_i), contribution to row l = y * b_i at l = i + k? Actually y*b: output_l = ∑_i y_{l-i} b_i. For fixed i, as y_{l-i} varies with l, y index r=l-i from 0..n-1, so l = i + r for r=0..n-1. Thus T[l,i] = y_{l-i} if 0<=l-i<n, i.e., l in [i, i+n-1]. Value y[l-i], not y[i]. Previous code `T[j:j+n, j] = a_fixed` assigned constant a_j to rows j..j+n-1, which corresponds to (a_fixed*b)_l for fixed b_j? Let's check: It assigned T[row, col j] = a_fixed[j], constant, not y[row-j]. That matrix computes ∑_j a_j b_j for all rows in interval? That is not convolution! Wait previous build_toeplitz:
```
T = np.zeros((2n-1,n))
for j in range(n):
    T[j:j+n, j] = a_fixed
```
This means T_{l,j} = a_j if l in [j, j+n-1], else 0. Then (T b)_l = ∑_{j: l in [j,j+n-1]} a_j b_j = ∑_{j} a_j b_j I(j<=l<j+n). This is not ∑ y_j b_{l-j}. It is like cumulative? Did previous code have a bug? They claimed `T@b = convolve(a_fixed,b)`. Let's verify with small n=2, y=[y0,y1], b=[b0,b1]. Convolution y*b = [y0 b0, y0 b1 + y1 b0, y1 b1]. Their T columns:
col0 rows0,1 = y0 => contrib [y0 b0, y0 b0,0]
col1 rows1,2 = y1 => contrib [0,y1 b1,y1 b1]

```python
"""
Search for nonnegative sequences minimizing
    2*n*max(convolve(a,a))/(sum(a))^2
using a blend of:
  - Fourier square-root/projection iterations driven by a clipped target
    coefficient vector,
  - full and top-k log-sum-exp gradient descent in softmax space,
  - coordinate/peak-transfer polishing,
  - a Nesterov-style LP best-response game,
  - length interpolation and final local refinement.
"""

import time
import numpy as np
from scipy.fft import rfft, irfft

try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog
except Exception:
    linprog = None


def next_pow2(x):
    x = int(x)
    if x <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (x - 1).bit_length()


def sanitize_sequence(a):
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    return arr


def normalize_positive(a):
    arr = sanitize_sequence(a)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    n = len(arr)
    if n <= 0 or not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-15:
        return np.ones(max(1, n), dtype=np.float64) / max(1, n)
    return arr / s


def fast_selfconv(a, ff=None):
    a = sanitize_sequence(a)
    n = len(a)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    if ff is None:
        ff = next_pow2(L)
    A = rfft(a, ff)
    c = irfft(A * A, ff)[:L]
    return np.maximum(c, 0.0)


def score_normalized(a):
    a = normalize_positive(a)
    if len(a) <= 0:
        return float("inf")
    c = fast_selfconv(a)
    sc = 2.0 * len(a) * float(c.max())
    return sc if np.isfinite(sc) else float("inf")


def softmax_fn(z):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64)
    z = z - float(np.max(z))
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-300:
        return np.ones(len(z), dtype=np.float64) / len(z)
    return e / s


def perturb_sequence(a, rng, scale):
    a = normalize_positive(a)
    b = a * (1.0 + rng.normal(0.0, float(scale), len(a)))
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    s = float(b.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-15:
        return a.copy()
    return b / s


def interpolate_sequence(a, new_n):
    a = normalize_positive(a)
    old_n = len(a)
    new_n = int(new_n)
    if old_n == new_n:
        return a.copy()
    old_x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, old_n)
    new_x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, new_n)
    b = np.interp(new_x, old_x, a)
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    s = float(b.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-15:
        return np.ones(new_n, dtype=np.float64) / new_n
    return b / s


def make_structured(n, kind):
    n = int(n)
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    if kind == 0:
        a = np.cos(0.5 * np.pi * x) ** 2
    elif kind == 1:
        a = np.exp(-4.0 * (x - 0.5) ** 2)
    elif kind == 2:
        a = np.sin(np.pi * x) ** 3
    elif kind == 3:
        a = (1.0 - x) ** 2
    elif kind == 4:
        y = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi, n)
        a = 0.7 + 0.2 * np.cos(2.0 * y) + 0.1 * np.cos(5.0 * y)
    elif kind == 5:
        a = np.exp(-8.0 * (x - 0.25) ** 2) + 0.6 * np.exp(-8.0 * (x - 0.75) ** 2)
    elif kind == 6:
        a = np.sin(np.pi * x) * (np.cos(np.pi * x / 3.0) ** 2)
    elif kind == 7:
        y = np.linspace(-1.0, 1.0, n)
        a = np.maximum(0.0, 1.0 - np.abs(y) ** 1.5)
    elif kind == 8:
        a = np.exp(-50.0 * x ** 2) + np.exp(-50.0 * (x - 1.0) ** 2)
    else:
        a = np.exp(-3.0 * (x - 0.5) ** 2)
    return normalize_positive(a)


def convolve_and_gradient_rel(a, ff, T_rel, topk=None):
    a = sanitize_sequence(a)
    n = len(a)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    A = rfft(a, ff)
    c = irfft(A * A, ff)[:L]
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    cmax = float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(cmax) or cmax <= 0.0:
        cmax = 1e-15
    T = max(1e-15, float(T_rel) * cmax)

    if topk is None:
        z = c / T
        zmax = float(z.max())
        ez = np.exp(z - zmax)
        se = float(ez.sum())
        if se <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(se):
            w = np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / L
            val = T * (zmax + np.log(L))
        else:
            w = ez / se
            val = T * (zmax + np.log(se))
    else:
        k = int(topk)
        if k < 1:
            k = 1
        if k >= L:
            idx = np.arange(L)
        else:
            idx = np.argpartition(c, -k)[-k:]
        vals = c[idx]
        z = vals / T
        zmax = float(z.max())
        ez = np.exp(z - zmax)
        se = float(ez.sum())
        if se <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(se):
            w = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
            w[idx] = 1.0 / len(idx)
            val = T * (zmax + np.log(len(idx)))
        else:
            w = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
            w[idx] = ez / se
            val = T * (zmax + np.log(se))

    W = rfft(w, ff)
    r = irfft(W * np.conj(A), ff)[:n]
    grad = 2.0 * r
    return c, float(2.0 * n * cmax), grad, float(val)


def run_z_lse(a0, time_limit, T_start_rel, T_end_rel, lr_start, lr_end,
              topk=None, seed=0):
    time_limit = float(time_limit)
    if time_limit <= 1.0:
        a = normalize_positive(a0)
        return a, score_normalized(a)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    a = normalize_positive(a0)
    n = len(a)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    ff = next_pow2(L)

    c = fast_selfconv(a, ff)
    best_score = float(2.0 * n * c.max())
    best = a.copy()

    z = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
    z -= float(np.max(z))
    m = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    t_adam = 0

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    t_end = t0 + time_limit
    last_print = t0
    it = 0
    no_improve = 0
    lr_scale = 1.0

    while True:
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now >= t_end - 0.5:
            break

        a = softmax_fn(z)
        frac = (now - t0) / max(time_limit, 1e-9)
        frac = min(1.0, max(0.0, frac))
        T_rel = T_start_rel + (T_end_rel - T_start_rel) * frac

        c, score, grad, _ = convolve_and_gradient_rel(a, ff, T_rel, topk)

        if np.isfinite(score) and score < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score = score
            best = a.copy()
            no_improve = 0
            if now - last_print > 5.0:
                print(f"      z-LSE: score={best_score:.8f}, n={n}, t={now - t0:.0f}s")
                last_print = now
        else:
            no_improve += 1

        if no_improve == 300:
            lr_scale = max(0.2, lr_scale * 0.7)
            no_improve = 0

        if no_improve > 5000:
            scale = 0.05 + 0.25 * min(1.0, frac)
            a_p = perturb_sequence(best, rng, scale)
            z = np.log(np.maximum(a_p, 1e-300))
            z -= float(np.max(z))
            m[:] = 0.0
            v[:] = 0.0
            t_adam = 0
            lr_scale = 1.0
            no_improve = 0
            it += 1
            continue

        S = float(np.dot(a, grad))
        grad_z = a * (grad - S)

        lr = (lr_start + (lr_end - lr_start) * frac) * lr_scale
        t_adam += 1
        m *= 0.9
        m += 0.1 * grad_z
        g2 = grad_z * grad_z
        v *= 0.999
        v += 0.001 * g2

        m_hat = m / (1.0 - 0.9 ** t_adam)
        v_hat = v / (1.0 - 0.999 ** t_adam)
        z -= lr * m_hat / (np.sqrt(v_hat) + 1e-8)
        z -= float(np.max(z))
        it += 1

    return best, best_score


def clip_to_box_sum(c, M):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    s = float(c.sum())
    if s > 0.0 and np.isfinite(s):
        c /= s
    M = float(M)
    if M <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(M):
        return c / max(1.0, c.sum())

    T = c.copy()
    for _ in range(30):
        excess = T - M
        pos = excess > 0.0
        if not np.any(pos):
            break
        ex = float(excess[pos].sum())
        if ex < 1e-14:
            break
        T[pos] -= excess[pos]
        deficit = 1.0 - float(T.sum())
        if abs(deficit) < 1e-14:
            break
        room = M - T
        room = np.maximum(room, 0.0)
        rs = float(room.sum())
        if rs < 1e-14:
            break
        T += deficit * room / rs

    T = np.maximum(T, 0.0)
    s = float(T.sum())
    if s < 1e-14 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.ones_like(T, dtype=np.float64) / len(T)
    return T / s


def adjust_phase_half(C_half, A_half, ff):
    magC = np.abs(C_half)
    sqrtC = np.sqrt(magC)
    A2 = A_half * A_half

    with np.errstate(divide="ignore", invalid="ignore"):
        ratio = C_half / A2
    delta = np.angle(ratio)
    argA = np.angle(A_half)
    phi = argA + 0.5 * delta

    small = np.abs(A_half) < 1e-15
    if np.any(small):
        phi[small] = 0.5 * np.angle(C_half[small])

    bad = ~np.isfinite(phi)
    if np.any(bad):
        phi[bad] = 0.5 * np.angle(C_half[bad])

    out = sqrtC * np.exp(1j * phi)
    out[0] = float(np.sqrt(max(float(np.real(C_half[0])), 0.0)))

    if ff % 2 == 0:
        sign = -1.0 if float(np.real(A_half[-1])) < 0.0 else 1.0
        out[-1] = sign * float(np.sqrt(max(float(np.abs(C_half[-1])), 0.0)))

    bad_out = ~np.isfinite(out)
    if np.any(bad_out):
        out[bad_out] = 0.0
    return out


def add_phase_noise(A_half, rng, level, ff):
    if len(A_half) <= 1:
        return A_half
    out = np.array(A_half, dtype=np.complex128, copy=True)
    noise = rng.normal(0.0, float(level), len(A_half))
    noise[0] = 0.0
    if ff % 2 == 0:
        noise[-1] = 0.0
    out *= np.exp(1j * noise)
    out[0] = abs(out[0])
    if ff % 2 == 0:
        out[-1] = float(out[-1].real)
    return out


def fourier_flat_search(a0, time_limit, ratio_end=1.25, seed=0):
    time_limit = float(time_limit)
    if time_limit <= 2.0:
        a = normalize_positive(a0)
        return a, score_normalized(a)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    a = normalize_positive(a0)
    n = len(a)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    ff = next_pow2(L)
    cmean = 1.0 / float(L)

    a_pad = np.zeros(ff, dtype=np.float64)
    T_pad = np.zeros(ff, dtype=np.float64)
    a_pad[:n] = a
    A = rfft(a_pad, ff)
    c = np.maximum(irfft(A * A, ff)[:L], 0.0)
    cs = float(c.sum())
    if cs > 0.0:
        c /= cs
    cmax = float(c.max())
    best_score = float(2.0 * n * cmax) if np.isfinite(cmax) else float("inf")
    best = a.copy()

    ratio_init = 1.0
    if cmean > 0.0 and np.isfinite(cmax):
        ratio_init = max(1.0, cmax / cmean)

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    t_end = t0 + time_limit
    last_print = t0
    it = 0
    no_improve = 0

    while True:
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now >= t_end - 0.5:
            break

        c = np.maximum(irfft(A * A, ff)[:L], 0.0)
        cs = float(c.sum())
        if cs > 0.0:
            c /= cs
        cmax = float(c.max())
        score = float(2.0 * n * cmax) if np.isfinite(cmax) else float("inf")

        if np.isfinite(score) and score < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score = score
            best = a.copy()
            no_improve = 0
            if now - last_print > 5.0:
                print(f"      Fourier: score={best_score:.8f}, n={n}, t={now - t0:.0f}s")
                last_print = now
        else:
            no_improve += 1

        if no_improve > 8000:
            a = best.copy()
            a_pad[:n] = a
            a_pad[n:] = 0.0
            A = rfft(a_pad, ff)
            no_improve = 0
            it += 1
            continue

        frac = min(1.0, max(0.0, (now - t0) / time_limit))
        target_ratio = ratio_init + (float(ratio_end) - ratio_init) * frac
        if target_ratio < 1.02:
            target_ratio = 1.02
        M = cmean * target_ratio

        T = clip_to_box_sum(c, M)
        T_pad[:L] = T
        T_pad[L:] = 0.0
        C = rfft(T_pad, ff)

        Anew = adjust_phase_half(C, A, ff)
        if no_improve > 500 or (it % 500 == 0 and rng.random() < 0.25):
            level = 0.05 + 0.20 * min(1.0, no_improve / 10000.0)
            Anew = add_phase_noise(Anew, rng, level, ff)

        a_full = irfft(Anew, ff)
        pos = np.maximum(a_full, 0.0)

        pos2 = np.concatenate((pos, pos))
        cs2 = np.empty(2 * ff + 1, dtype=np.float64)
        cs2[0] = 0.0
        cs2[1:] = np.cumsum(pos2)
        sums = cs2[n:n + ff] - cs2[:ff]
        s_idx = int(np.argmax(sums))
        a_new = np.roll(pos, -s_idx)[:n]
        a_new = np.where(np.isfinite(a_new), a_new, 0.0)
        sn = float(a_new.sum())

        accept = False
        if sn > 1e-10:
            a_new = a_new / sn
            A_check = rfft(a_new, ff)
            c_check = np.maximum(irfft(A_check * A_check, ff)[:L], 0.0)
            cc = float(c_check.sum())
            if cc > 0.0:
                c_check /= cc
            score_check = float(2.0 * n * c_check.max())

            if np.isfinite(score_check):
                if score_check < best_score - 1e-12:
                    best_score = score_check
                    best = a_new.copy()
                    no_improve = 0
                    accept = True
                    if now - last_print > 5.0:
                        print(f"      Fourier: score={best_score:.8f}, n={n}, t={now - t0:.0f}s")
                        last_print = now
                elif np.isfinite(score) and score_check < 1.25 * score:
                    accept = True
                elif no_improve > 2000 and score_check < 2.0 * best_score:
                    accept = True

                if accept:
                    a = a_new
                    a_pad[:n] = a
                    a_pad[n:] = 0.0
                    A = A_check
            else:
                accept = False

        it += 1

    return best, best_score


def correlation_of_weights(w, a, ff):
    W = rfft(w, ff)
    A = rfft(a, ff)
    return irfft(W * np.conj(A), ff)[:len(a)]


def coordinate_polish(a0, time_limit, seed=0):
    time_limit = float(time_limit)
    if time_limit <= 1.0:
        a = normalize_positive(a0)
        return a, score_normalized(a)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    a = normalize_positive(a0)
    n = len(a)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    ff = next_pow2(L)

    c = fast_selfconv(a, ff)
    current_score = float(2.0 * n * c.max())
    best = a.copy()
    best_score = current_score

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    t_end = t0 + time_limit
    last_print = t0
    stall = 0
    fractions = (0.005, 0.02, 0.08, 0.20, 0.50)

    while True:
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now >= t_end - 0.5:
            break

        cmax = float(c.max())
        if not np.isfinite(cmax) or cmax <= 0.0:
            a = best.copy()
            c = fast_selfconv(a, ff)
            current_score = float(2.0 * n * c.max())
            continue

        if np.isfinite(current_score) and current_score < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score = current_score
            best = a.copy()

        K = max(20, min(80, n // 10))
        if K >= L:
            top_idx = np.arange(L)
        else:
            top_idx = np.argsort(c)[::-1][:K]
        vals = c[top_idx]
        Tw = max(1e-15, 0.05 * cmax)
        zv = vals / Tw
        zv -= float(zv.max())
        ez = np.exp(zv)
        se = float(ez.sum())
        if se <= 0.0:
            wtop = np.ones_like(vals) / len(vals)
        else:
            wtop = ez / se
        w = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        w[top_idx] = wtop
        S = correlation_of_weights(w, a, ff)

        min_a = 1e-14
        src_all = np.where(a > min_a)[0]
        if len(src_all) > 0:
            order = np.argsort(S[src_all])
            src_subset = src_all[order[-30:]]
        else:
            src_subset = np.empty(0, dtype=int)

        dest_all = np.argsort(S)
        dest_subset = dest_all[:80]

        candidates = []
        for s_idx in src_subset:
            sv = S[s_idx]
            for d_idx in dest_subset:
                if d_idx == s_idx:
                    continue
                val = S[d_idx] - sv
                if val < -1e-15:
                    candidates.append((float(val), int(s_idx), int(d_idx)))

        for k in top_idx[:10]:
            js = max(0, k - (n - 1))
            je = min(n - 1, k)
            if js > je:
                continue
            idx = np.arange(js, je + 1)
            prod = a[idx] * a[k - idx]
            im = int(np.argmax(prod))
            src1 = int(idx[im])
            partner = int(k - src1)
            if len(dest_subset) > 0:
                d0 = int(dest_subset[0])
                for src2 in (src1, partner):
                    if 0 <= src2 < n and src2 != d0:
                        candidates.append((float(S[d0] - S[src2]), src2, d0))

        seen = set()
        uniq = []
        for val, s, d in candidates:
            key = (s, d)
            if key not in seen:
                seen.add(key)
                uniq.append((val, s, d))
        uniq.sort()
        uniq = uniq[:12]

        accepted = False
        for val, s, d in uniq:
            if a[s] <= 1e-15:
                continue
            for frac in fractions:
                amt = a[s] * frac
                a2 = a.copy()
                a2[s] -= amt
                a2[d] += amt
                if a2[s] < -1e-12:
                    continue
                a2 = np.maximum(a2, 0.0)
                s2 = float(a2.sum())
                if s2 < 1e-12:
                    continue
                a2 /= s2
                c2 = fast_selfconv(a2, ff)
                sc2 = float(2.0 * n * c2.max())
                if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < current_score - 1e-13:
                    a = a2
                    c = c2
                    current_score = sc2
                    accepted = True
                    stall = 0
                    if sc2 < best_score - 1e-12:
                        best_score = sc2
                        best = a.copy()
                        if now - last_print > 5.0:
                            print(f"      Coord: score={best_score:.8f}, n={n}, t={now - t0:.0f}s")
                            last_print = now
                    break
            if accepted:
                break

        if not accepted:
            stall += 1
            if stall > 50:
                improved_random = False
                if len(src_subset) > 0 and len(dest_subset) > 0:
                    s = int(src_subset[rng.integers(0, len(src_subset))])
                    d = int(dest_subset[rng.integers(0, len(dest_subset))])
                    if s != d and a[s] > 1e-15:
                        for frac in (0.002, 0.01, 0.05):
                            amt = a[s] * frac
                            a2 = a.copy()
                            a2[s] -= amt
                            a2[d] += amt
                            a2 = np.maximum(a2, 0.0)
                            s2 = float(a2.sum())
                            if s2 < 1e-12:
                                continue
                            a2 /= s2
                            c2 = fast_selfconv(a2, ff)
                            sc2 = float(2.0 * n * c2.max())
                            if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < current_score - 1e-13:
                                a = a2
                                c = c2
                                current_score = sc2
                                if sc2 < best_score - 1e-12:
                                    best_score = sc2
                                    best = a.copy()
                                improved_random = True
                                break
                if not improved_random:
                    a = perturb_sequence(best, rng, 0.01)
                    c = fast_selfconv(a, ff)
                    current_score = float(2.0 * n * c.max())
                    if current_score < best_score - 1e-12:
                        best_score = current_score
                        best = a.copy()
                stall = 0

            if stall > 1000:
                break

    return best, best_score


def nesterov_lp_game(a0, time_limit, seed=0):
    time_limit = float(time_limit)
    if time_limit <= 30.0 or linprog is None:
        a = normalize_positive(a0)
        return a, score_normalized(a)

    a0 = normalize_positive(a0)
    n = len(a0)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    if n > 2000:
        return a0, score_normalized(a0)

    try:
        Tmat = np.zeros((L, n + 1), dtype=np.float64)
        Tmat[:, n] = -1.0
        c_obj = np.zeros(n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
        c_obj[-1] = 1.0
        b_ub = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        A_eq = np.ones((1, n + 1), dtype=np.float64)
        A_eq[0, n] = 0.0
        b_eq = np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
        bounds = [(0.0, None)] * (n + 1)
    except Exception:
        a = normalize_positive(a0)
        return a, score_normalized(a)

    y = normalize_positive(a0)
    a_nest = y.copy()
    best = a_nest.copy()
    c = fast_selfconv(best)
    best_score = float(2.0 * n * c.max())

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    t_end = t0 + time_limit
    last_print = t0
    t = 1
    no_improve = 0
    fail = 0

    while True:
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now >= t_end - 5.0:
            break

        for j in range(n):
            Tmat[j:j + n, j] = y

        per_time = max(1.0, min(8.0, (t_end - now) * 0.1))
        res = None
        try:
            res = linprog(
                c_obj,
                A_ub=Tmat,
                b_ub=b_ub,
                A_eq=A_eq,
                b_eq=b_eq,
                bounds=bounds,
                method="highs",
                options={"time_limit": per_time,
                         "dual_feasibility_tolerance": 1e-8,
                         "primal_feasibility_tolerance": 1e-8}
            )
        except Exception:
            try:
                res = linprog(
                    c_obj,
                    A_ub=Tmat,
                    b_ub=b_ub,
                    A_eq=A_eq,
                    b_eq=b_eq,
                    bounds=bounds,
                    options={"time_limit": per_time}
                )
            except Exception:
                res = None

        if res is None or not getattr(res, "success", False):
            fail += 1
            if fail > 5:
                break
            continue

        b = np.asarray(res.x[:n], dtype=np.float64)
        b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
        sb = float(b.sum())
        if sb < 1e-12:
            b = y.copy()
        else:
            b /= sb

        alpha = 2.0 / (t + 2.0)
        y_new = (1.0 - alpha) * y + alpha * b
        y_new = np.maximum(y_new, 0.0)
        sy = float(y_new.sum())
        if sy < 1e-12:
            y_new = b.copy()
        else:
            y_new /= sy

        if t == 1:
            a_new = b.copy()
        else:
            momentum = (t - 1.0) / (t + 2.0)
            a_new = y_new + momentum * (y_new - y)
            a_new = np.maximum(a_new, 0.0)
            sa = float(a_new.sum())
            if sa < 1e-12:
                a_new = y_new.copy()
            else:
                a_new /= sa

        y = y_new
        a_nest = a_new

        improved = False
        for cand in (a_nest, y):
            cc = fast_selfconv(cand)
            sc = float(2.0 * n * cc.max())
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-12:
                best_score = sc
                best = cand.copy()
                improved = True
                if now - last_print > 5.0:
                    print(f"      LP-game: score={best_score:.8f}, n={n}, iter={t}, t={now - t0:.0f}s")
                    last_print = now

        if improved:
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1

        if no_improve > 100:
            y = best.copy()
            a_nest = best.copy()
            t = 1
            no_improve = 0
            fail = 0

        t += 1

    return best, best_score


def get_parent_sequence():
    try:
        seq = height_sequence_1
    except Exception:
        return None
    if seq is None:
        return None
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
        return None
    if arr.ndim != 1 or arr.size == 0:
        return None
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-10:
        return None
    return arr / s


def propose_candidate():
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    TOTAL = 1750.0

    best = None
    best_score = float("inf")

    parent = get_parent_sequence()
    if parent is not None:
        best = parent
        best_score = score_normalized(best)
        print(f"Parent initialized: n={len(best)}, fast_score={best_score:.8f}")
    else:
        n = 1600
        best = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
        best_score = score_normalized(best)
        print("No parent, using uniform n=1600 initialization.")

    rng = np.random.default_rng(2025)

    if best is not None:
        variants = []
        variants.append(best.copy())
        variants.append(best[::-1].copy())
        sym = best + best[::-1]
        sym = sym / max(1e-30, float(sym.sum()))
        variants.append(sym)
        base_n = len(best)
        for kind in range(10):
            variants.append(make_structured(base_n, kind))
        for v in variants:
            sc = score_normalized(v)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best = v
                best_score = sc

    remaining = TOTAL - (time.monotonic() - t_start)
    slice_time = min(0.18 * remaining, 350.0)
    if slice_time > 30.0 and best is not None:
        a, sc = fourier_flat_search(best, slice_time, ratio_end=1.30, seed=101)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best = a
            best_score = sc
            print(f"After Fourier main: {best_score:.8f}, n={len(best)}")

    remaining = TOTAL - (time.monotonic() - t_start)
    slice_time = min(0.22 * remaining, 450.0)
    if slice_time > 20.0 and best is not None:
        a, sc = run_z_lse(best, slice_time, 0.8, 0.02, 0.06, 0.004, None, seed=102)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best = a
            best_score = sc
            print(f"After main z-LSE: {best_score:.8f}, n={len(best)}")

    remaining = TOTAL - (time.monotonic() - t_start)
    if best is not None and len(best) <= 1800:
        slice_time = min(0.15 * remaining, 250.0)
        if slice_time > 40.0:
            a, sc = nesterov_lp_game(best, slice_time, seed=103)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best = a
                best_score = sc
                print(f"After LP game: {best_score:.8f}, n={len(best)}")

    remaining = TOTAL - (time.monotonic() - t_start)
    slice_time = min(0.12 * remaining, 220.0)
    if slice_time > 30.0 and best is not None:
        a, sc = fourier_flat_search(best, slice_time, ratio_end=1.15, seed=104)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best = a
            best_score = sc
            print(f"After Fourier sub: {best_score:.8f}, n={len(best)}")

    remaining = TOTAL - (time.monotonic() - t_start)
    slice_time = min(0.12 * remaining, 220.0)
    if slice_time > 20.0 and best is not None:
        a, sc = coordinate_polish(best, slice_time, seed=105)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best = a
            best_score = sc
            print(f"After coordinate polish: {best_score:.8f}, n={len(best)}")

    if best is not None:
        base_n = len(best)
        targets = []
        if base_n < 2000:
            targets.append(2000)
        if base_n < 2400:
            targets.append(2400)
        if base_n < 2800:
            targets.append(2800)

        for new_n in targets[:2]:
            remaining = TOTAL - (time.monotonic() - t_start)
            if remaining < 120.0:
                break
            init = interpolate_sequence(best, new_n)
            sc_init = score_normalized(init)
            if np.isfinite(sc_init) and sc_init < best_score:
                best = init
                best_score = sc_init

            slice_time = min(0.12 * remaining, 200.0)
            if slice_time > 30.0:
                a, sc = run_z_lse(best, slice_time, 0.8, 0.03, 0.05, 0.003, None,
                                  seed=110 + (new_n % 100))
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                    best = a
                    best_score = sc

            remaining = TOTAL - (time.monotonic() - t_start)
            if remaining > 60.0:
                slice_time = min(0.04 * remaining, 60.0)
                if slice_time > 10.0:
                    a, sc = coordinate_polish(best, slice_time, seed=120 + (new_n % 100))
                    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                        best = a
                        best_score = sc
            if best is not None:
                print(f"After length {len(best)}: {best_score:.8f}")

    remaining = TOTAL - (time.monotonic() - t_start)
    for i in range(3):
        if remaining < 50.0 or best is None:
            break
        scale = 0.05 * (i + 1)
        init = perturb_sequence(best, np.random.default_rng(200 + i), scale)
        slice_time = min(remaining / max(1, 3 - i), 120.0)
        if slice_time > 20.0:
            a, sc = run_z_lse(init, slice_time, 0.4, 0.01, 0.05, 0.002, 40, seed=210 + i)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best = a
                best_score = sc
                print(f"After multi {i}: {best_score:.8f}, n={len(best)}")

    remaining = TOTAL - (time.monotonic() - t_start)
    slice_time = min(0.5 * remaining, 120.0)
    if slice_time > 15.0 and best is not None:
        a, sc = run_z_lse(best, slice_time, 0.2, 0.005, 0.03, 0.001, 20, seed=300)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best = a
            best_score = sc
            print(f"After final LSE: {best_score:.8f}, n={len(best)}")

    remaining = TOTAL - (time.monotonic() - t_start)
    slice_time = min(max(0.0, remaining - 15.0), 60.0)
    if slice_time > 5.0 and best is not None:
        a, sc = coordinate_polish(best, slice_time, seed=400)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best = a
            best_score = sc
            print(f"After final coordinate: {best_score:.8f}, n={len(best)}")

    if best is None:
        best = np.ones(1600, dtype=np.float64) / 1600

    finalists = []
    finalists.append(best)
    finalists.append(best[::-1])
    sym = best + best[::-1]
    sym = sym / max(1e-30, float(sym.sum()))
    finalists.append(sym)

    best_out = None
    best_eval = float("inf")
    for f in finalists:
        try:
            out = [float(x) for x in f]
            out = [max(0.0, min(1000.0, x)) for x in out]
            sc = float(evaluate_sequence(out))
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_eval:
                best_eval = sc
                best_out = out
        except Exception:
            pass

    if best_out is None:
        best_out = [float(max(0.0, min(1000.0, x))) for x in best]

    elapsed = time.monotonic() - t_start
    print(f"Final fast score={best_score:.8f}, evaluator score={best_eval:.8f}, "
          f"n={len(best_out)}, elapsed={elapsed:.0f}s")
    return best_out
```
